Party documents and direct records

AT2026-10-25#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Heimaturlaub statt Absitzen der Haftstrafe

FPÖ MP Christian Lausch criticized the coalition's prison reform bill in parliament, arguing it would lead to early releases rather than deportations and noting that Justice Minister Anna Sporrer has not concluded any necessary repatriation agreements in 20 months.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Was Sie mit dieser Strafvollzugsnovelle vorhaben, bedeutet lediglich früheres Entlassen, späteres Einsperren und die Abschaffung jeder Abschreckung. Das ist nicht unser Verständnis einer Strafvollzugsnovelle. So etwas werden wir nicht mittragen!
What you intend with this prison reform bill means merely earlier releases, later imprisonment and the abolition of any deterrence. That is not our understanding of a prison reform bill. We will not support such a thing!
Christian Lausch
Wir wollen die Rückführung und die Strafverbüßung im Heimatland. Dann brauchen wir auch keine neuen Gefängnisse zu bauen, für die ohnehin kein Geld da ist. Wenn es gelingt, 2.000 ausländische Straftäter zurückzuführen, sparen wir uns bei einem konservativ gerechneten Hafttag von 150 Euro rund 110 Millionen Euro an Steuergeld pro Jahr
We want repatriation and serving of sentences in the home country. Then we also don't need to build new prisons, for which there is no money anyway. If we succeed in repatriating 2,000 foreign criminals, we save ourselves around 110 million euros in tax money per year at a conservatively calculated detention day of 150 euros
Christian Lausch

AT2026-10-24#
party document Policy proposal

Angebliche ORF-Sparpläne sind reine Augenauswischerei

FPÖ media spokesperson Christian Hafenecker criticized the ORF's announced plan to reduce the number of employees earning over 170,000 euros annually from 65 to 60 as cosmetic and called for support of the FPÖ's ORF referendum (ORF-Volksbegehren) to fundamentally reform public broadcasting.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Dass ausgerechnet jene ORF-Chefetage, die mit über 700 Millionen Euro Einnahmen pro Jahr aus der ORF-Haushaltsabgabe nicht auskommt, die für die explodierenden Kosten und den aufgeblähten Apparat verantwortlich ist, nun den großen Sparmeister spielen will, ist an Zynismus nicht zu überbieten. Da macht sich der Bock zum Gärtner
That precisely those ORF executives who cannot manage with over 700 million euros in annual revenue from the ORF household levy, who are responsible for the exploding costs and the bloated apparatus, now want to play the great savings master, cannot be surpassed in cynicism. The fox is guarding the henhouse
Christian Hafenecker
Die unverschämten Gehälter einiger weniger werden marginal gekürzt, während der Wasserkopf und die Ineffizienz im System unangetastet bleiben
The outrageous salaries of a few are being marginally cut, while the top-heavy structure and inefficiency in the system remain untouched
Christian Hafenecker

AT2026-10-23#
party document Public statement

Entwurf zur Sozialhilfe-Reform ist ein Pfusch

FPÖ social affairs spokesperson Dagmar Belakowitsch criticized the government coalition's draft social welfare reform as inadequate, arguing it fails to achieve nationwide standardization, forces some federal states to increase spending, allows Vienna to continue spending through in-kind benefits, and does not effectively tie social benefits to citizenship.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Wo soll hier bitte ein Paradigmenwechsel sein? Der eigentliche Grund für dieses monatelange Koalitions-Theater war das Versprechen, endlich eine bundesweite Vereinheitlichung zu schaffen – und genau diese wurde komplett verfehlt!
Where is there supposed to be a paradigm shift here? The actual reason for this months-long coalition theater was the promise to finally create nationwide standardization – and precisely this has been completely missed!
Dagmar Belakowitsch
Integration ist eine Bringschuld und kein kostenpflichtiges Belohnungsprogramm!
Integration is an obligation to deliver and not a paid reward program!
Dagmar Belakowitsch

The Geneva Citizens' Movement (MCG) defended its use of a modified political poster during a campaign against a 39.5 million franc credit to France, responding to plagiarism accusations from the original artist by arguing that political posters are public arguments subject to satire and reinterpretation.

party document Election or campaign

Am 29. November: Nein zur Erhöhung der Mehrwertsteuer

The Swiss Party of Labour calls for a No vote on the November 29 referendum to finance the 13th AHV pension through a VAT increase, arguing the regressive tax burdens lower-income households and that AHV reserves of 60.4 billion francs make the increase unnecessary.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die Lohnabhängigen in der Schweiz, die bereits unter den steigenden Treibstoffpreisen leiden, welche die Kriegspolitik der USA und ihrer Verbündeten verursachen, und eine weitere Erhöhung der Krankenkassenprämien für das Jahr 2027 schlucken müssen, sehen sich einem starken und unnötigen zusätzlichen Druck ausgesetzt.
Wage earners in Switzerland, who are already suffering from rising fuel prices caused by the war policy of the USA and its allies, and must swallow a further increase in health insurance premiums for 2027, are facing strong and unnecessary additional pressure.
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz
NEIN zur Finanzierung der AHV durch eine Erhöhung der Mehrwertsteuer am 29. November!
NO to financing the AHV through an increase in VAT on November 29!
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz

Nein zur Lockerung von Waffenexporten, Ja zur internationalen Solidarität!

The Swiss Party of Labour opposes the relaxation of war material export laws that would allow exports to 36 NATO states and allies even during armed conflicts and permit re-export, calling for a 'No' vote in the November 29 referendum.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die bürgerlichen Parteien möchten die militärische Zusammenarbeit mit dem westlichen, imperialistischen Block weiter ausbauen. Das müssen wir verhindern!
The bourgeois parties want to further expand military cooperation with the western, imperialist bloc. We must prevent this!
Alexander Eniline
Diese Lockerung fördert nicht nur den Militarismus und die Kriegsgefahr für die Schweizer Bevölkerung, sondern widerspricht auch dem Prinzip der internationalen Solidarität der Arbeiter:innenklasse.
This relaxation not only promotes militarism and the danger of war for the Swiss population, but also contradicts the principle of international solidarity of the working class.
Timeo Antognini

Progressive change in the country has content. And this content starts from ending these policies that have been implemented in recent years and have led to the sharpening of inequalities, the magnification of social problems and also to major deficits regarding social policy.

Νομοθετικές πρωτοβουλίες του ΑΚΕΛ για μείωση του κόστους ηλεκτρισμού

AKEL submitted three legislative proposals to the House of Representatives concerning the electricity market, aiming to recover excess profits from electricity producers, introduce competitive tenders for renewable energy projects, and limit the hoarding of renewable energy permits.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Κατατέθηκαν χθες ενώπιον της Ολομέλειας της Βουλής τρεις νέες προτάσεις νόμου του ΑΚΕΛ που αφορούν την αγορά ηλεκτρισμού, με στόχο την αντιμετώπιση των στρεβλώσεων, τον περιορισμό της αισχροκέρδειας και τη μείωση του κόστους ηλεκτρικής ενέργειας.
Three new legislative proposals by AKEL concerning the electricity market were submitted yesterday before the House Plenary, aiming to address distortions, limit profiteering, and reduce the cost of electricity.
AKEL
Προσβλέπουμε σε μια εποικοδομητική συζήτηση και θετική κατάληξη, ώστε η φθηνότερη παραγωγή ενέργειας από τις ΑΠΕ να μεταφράζεται επιτέλους σε φθηνότερο ηλεκτρισμό για τα νοικοκυριά και τις επιχειρήσεις.
We look forward to a constructive discussion and positive outcome, so that cheaper energy production from renewable sources finally translates into cheaper electricity for households and businesses.
AKEL

CY2026-10-22#
party document Policy proposal

Ο μέσος μισθός ανεβαίνει, αλλά για χιλιάδες εργαζόμενους ο μήνας δεν βγαίνει

AKEL calls for substantial minimum wage increases indexed to inflation, expansion of collective bargaining coverage through pending legislation per EU directive, mandatory application of sectoral agreements, and requirement that state subsidies and public contracts enforce collective agreements, arguing that median wages of €1,953 leave thousands of workers unable to meet basic costs.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Το ΑΚΕΛ επιμένει στην ουσιαστική αύξηση του κατώτατου μισθού, ώστε να ανταποκρίνεται στο πραγματικό κόστος ζωής, στην ετήσια αναπροσαρμογή του με βάση την ΑΤΑ και στον καθορισμό της ωριαίας απόδοσής του.
AKEL insists on a substantial increase of the minimum wage, so that it corresponds to the real cost of living, on its annual adjustment based on the CPI and on the determination of its hourly rate.
AKEL
Γι' αυτό επιβάλλεται η κυβέρνηση να προχωρήσει χωρίς άλλη καθυστέρηση στην κατάθεση του νομοσχεδίου για την αύξηση του αριθμού των εργαζομένων που καλύπτονται από Συλλογικές Συμβάσεις Εργασίας, όπως προνοεί άλλωστε και η σχετική Ευρωπαϊκή Οδηγία.
For this reason, the government must proceed without further delay to submit the bill to increase the number of workers covered by Collective Labor Agreements, as provided for by the relevant European Directive.
AKEL

CY2026-10-22#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Αγορά κυπριακής γης από ξένα συμφέροντα: Ώρα για κανόνες και περιορισμούς

AKEL MP Aristos Damianou announced that the Parliamentary Interior Committee has restarted discussions on regulating foreign acquisition of Cypriot land, with the party pushing for legislation by year-end that would cover not only individuals but also companies and funds purchasing property.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Σήμερα στην Επιτροπή Εσωτερικών επανεκκίνησαμε η συζήτηση ενός πολύ σοβαρού ζητήματος, που άπτεται και της κρατικής ασφάλειας και της οικονομίας, αλλά και του μέλλοντος αυτού του τόπου. Αυτό της απόκτησης ακίνητης ιδιοκτησίας, κυπριακής γης, από ξένα συμφέροντα.
Today in the Interior Committee we restarted the discussion of a very serious issue, which concerns state security and the economy, but also the future of this country. That of the acquisition of immovable property, Cypriot land, by foreign interests.
Aristos Damianou
Είναι η θέση μας, και είναι κατηγορηματική, ότι αν δεν ρυθμιστούν και τα θέματα των φυσικών προσώπων, των εταιρειών και των ταμείων και όλων εκείνων οι οποίοι με συγκαλυμμένο τρόπο αγοράζουν στην Κύπρο, τότε η ρύθμιση μόνο των φυσικών προσώπων θα είναι ελλειμματική.
It is our position, and it is categorical, that if the issues of natural persons, companies and funds and all those who buy in Cyprus in a concealed manner are not regulated, then the regulation of only natural persons will be deficient.
Aristos Damianou

The AfD federal party issued a statement on 21 October 2026 declaring it will not cooperate with media reporting based on allegedly stolen party data from a digital attack, calling the data theft a crime and warning that some leaked content appears to be falsified.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die AfD ist eine Rechtsstaatspartei. Entsprechend eng werden wir mit den staatlichen Stellen auf Bundes- und Landesebene zusammenarbeiten, um jene zur Rechenschaft zu ziehen, die unsere Partei digital angegriffen und Daten gestohlen haben.
The AfD is a rule-of-law party. Accordingly, we will work closely with state authorities at federal and state level to hold accountable those who digitally attacked our party and stole data.
Alternative for Germany
Die AfD-Daten wurden illegal kopiert. Das ist eine Straftat. All jene, die nun diese Daten nutzen, heißen ein Verbrechen gut und missachten oftmals das Persönlichkeitsrecht.
The AfD data was illegally copied. That is a criminal offense. All those who now use this data are condoning a crime and often disregarding personal rights.
Alternative for Germany

AfD zum Daten-Hack: Angriff auf Parteiensystem und Demokratie

The AfD issued a statement on October 20, 2026, responding to a September 2026 hacking attack on a hosting provider that resulted in stolen data being published on an activist website, calling it an illegal attack on the party system and democracy and stating they have notified authorities and data protection officials.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Seit gestern zeigt sich das Ausmaß des digitalen Angriffs: Obwohl nur wenige Mail-Postfächer widerrechtlich kopiert wurden, sind große Datenmengen abgeflossen.

Alternative für Deutschland

Such a digital attack is illegal and criminally relevant. Moreover, it is an attack on the German party system and thus on democracy.
Alternative für Deutschland

DE2026-10-20#
party document Public statement

Tino Chrupalla: Weitsichtige Standortpolitik und sozialer Frieden statt Pariser Zustände in Berlin

AfD federal spokesman Tino Chrupalla criticized the planned SPD-Left-Green coalition in Berlin, warning against social unrest similar to protests in Paris and other European cities, and called for economic policy and migration control to maintain social peace, citing AfD's approach in Saxony-Anhalt as a model.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Eine rot-grün-rote Koalition im Roten Rathaus wäre ein Fanal für Pariser Zustände in der deutschen Hauptstadt.
A red-green-red coalition in the Red City Hall would be a signal for Parisian conditions in the German capital.
Tino Chrupalla
Die Regierungen in Bund und Ländern müssen den Vertrauensverlust verhindern und dem Kontrollverlust vorbeugen. Mit weitsichtiger Standortpolitik müssen sie den Bürgern Perspektiven bieten, mit Augenmaß in der Migrationspolitik den sozialen Frieden sichern.
The governments at federal and state level must prevent the loss of trust and forestall the loss of control. With far-sighted economic policy they must offer citizens prospects, with moderation in migration policy secure social peace.
Tino Chrupalla

DE2026-10-19#
party document Public statement

Tino Chrupalla: Die Industrie wandert ab, die Bundesregierung schaut zu

AfD federal spokesman Tino Chrupalla criticized the federal government's inaction on industrial relocation abroad, calling for lower labor costs, less bureaucracy, lower taxes, education reform, a migration turnaround, reduced development aid, peace policy between Russia and Ukraine, and an end to the current form of energy transition.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Diese Zahlen sind ein Alarmsignal, doch die Bundesregierung bleibt untätig. Der aktuelle kleine Aufschwung ist mit Milliarden an Sonderschulden teuer erkauft.
These numbers are an alarm signal, but the federal government remains inactive. The current small upswing has been bought dearly with billions in special debts.
Tino Chrupalla
Produzieren in Deutschland muss sich wieder lohnen: mit niedrigeren Lohnnebenkosten, weniger Bürokratie, niedrigeren Steuern und einem Bildungssystem, das Fachkräfte statt Schulabbrecher hervorbringt.
Producing in Germany must be worthwhile again: with lower non-wage labor costs, less bureaucracy, lower taxes and an education system that produces skilled workers instead of school dropouts.
Tino Chrupalla

Left Alliance MP Timo Furuholm challenges Finns Party MPs from Southwest Finland to clarify whether they support their party's proposal to cut 600 million euros from municipal index increases and other municipal funding.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

FI2026-10-23#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Laura Meriluoto: Asuinpaikka ei saa ratkaista oikeutta terveydenhuollon asiakasmaksujen huojennuksiin

Left Alliance vice-chair and MP Laura Meriluoto is collecting signatures in parliament for a legislative initiative to expand wellbeing services areas' obligation to reduce or waive social and healthcare client fees, addressing regional disparities where twelve wellbeing services areas and Helsinki do not allow any reductions for flat-rate healthcare fees.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Orpo-Purran hallitus on monella tavalla romuttanut suomalaisen yhteiskunnan tasa-arvoa. Yksi räikeimmistä esimerkeistä on sosiaali- ja terveydenhuollon asiakasmaksujen enimmäismäärien roimat korotukset.
The Orpo-Purra government has in many ways destroyed the equality of Finnish society. One of the most blatant examples is the massive increases in the maximum amounts of social and healthcare client fees.
Laura Meriluoto
Kahdellatoista hyvinvointialueella ja Helsingissä terveydenhuollon tasasuuruisiin asiakasmaksuihin ei voi hakea lainkaan alennusta tai perimättä jättämistä. Asiakasmaksun huojentaminen ei saa riippua siitä, minkä hyvinvointialueen järjestämiä palveluja henkilö käyttää.
In twelve wellbeing services areas and Helsinki, one cannot apply for any reduction or waiver of flat-rate healthcare client fees. The reduction of client fees must not depend on which wellbeing services area's services a person uses.
Laura Meriluoto

FI2026-10-23#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Timo Furuholm: Sosiaaliturvan leikkaukset ja terveydenhuollon asiakasmaksujen korotukset vievät suomalaisia todella ahtaalle

Opposition parties including Left Alliance submitted an interpellation to the Orpo-Purra government regarding rising living costs, with MP Timo Furuholm criticizing social security cuts and healthcare fee increases and calling for restoration of basic security protections and affordable housing construction.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Hallitus voisi aloittaa suomalaisten elämän helpottamisen esimerkiksi hautaamalla nyt täällä eduskunnassa käsittelyssä olevat "Rydmanin hinnat" eli terveydenhuollon asiakasmaksujen korotukset ja uudet maksut. Suomessa hyvä terveys ei saa olla kiinni lompakon paksuudesta.
The government could start making life easier for Finns by, for example, burying the so-called 'Rydman prices' currently being processed in parliament, namely the healthcare fee increases and new charges. In Finland, good health must not depend on the thickness of one's wallet.
Timo Furuholm
Lisäksi nyt pitäisi saada perusturva kuntoon, palauttaa suojaosat ja lapsikorotukset sekä potkaista täysin pysähtynyt sosiaalinen asuinrakentaminen taas käyntiin, jotta ihmisillä on tulevaisuudessakin kohtuuhintaisia koteja.
In addition, basic security must now be fixed, protected portions and child supplements restored, and completely stalled social housing construction kicked back into action so that people will have affordable homes in the future as well.
Timo Furuholm

5 uutta vaalikoordinaattoria aloitti vasemmistoliitossa

Left Alliance appointed five new electoral coordinators across Finland to prepare for the spring 2027 parliamentary elections, with coordinators assigned to Uusimaa, Pohjanmaa, Pohjois-Pohjanmaa, and Häme regions.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Eduskuntavaaleissa tavoittelemme vahvaa tulosta kaikkialla Suomessa. Uudet vaalikoordinaattorimme ovat tärkeässä roolissa siinä, että eri alueiden ehdokkaat, aktiivit ja vapaaehtoiset pääsevät tekemään yhdessä voittavaa kampanjaa
In the parliamentary elections we are aiming for a strong result everywhere in Finland. Our new electoral coordinators are in an important role in ensuring that candidates, activists and volunteers in different regions can work together on a winning campaign
Anna Mäkipää

FI2026-10-22#
party document Public statement

Aino-Kaisa Pekonen: Perussuomalaisten leikkauslista johtaisi tuhansien hoitajien ja opettajien irtisanomisiin

Left Alliance parliamentary group chair Aino-Kaisa Pekonen criticized the Finns Party's proposed cuts list, stating it would lead to thousands of layoffs among nurses and teachers and destroy welfare services.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Sakset kädessä tavallisille ihmisille virnuilevat perussuomalaiset ovat täällä taas. Tällä leikkauslistalla tuhottaisiin sosiaali- ja terveyspalveluita, leikeltäisiin turvaverkkoja rikki sekä pahennettaisiin työttömyyttä.
The Finns Party, grinning with scissors in hand at ordinary people, are here again. With this cuts list, social and health services would be destroyed, safety nets would be cut to pieces, and unemployment would be worsened.
Aino-Kaisa Pekonen
Suomen työttömyys on jo nyt Euroopan korkein ja kasvaa yhä. Päälle perussuomalaisten leikkauslista johtaisi tuhansien hoitajien ja opettajien irtisanomisiin. Suomalaisen työntekijän kohtalo ei perussuomalaisia paljoa paina.
Finland's unemployment is already the highest in Europe and continues to grow. On top of that, the Finns Party's cuts list would lead to the dismissal of thousands of nurses and teachers. The fate of Finnish workers does not weigh heavily on the Finns Party.
Aino-Kaisa Pekonen

FI2026-10-21#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Pia Lohikoski: Nuorten uskon tulevaisuuteen voi palauttaa vain vastuullinen politiikka

Left Alliance MP Pia Lohikoski delivered a group speech in parliament criticizing the government's report on youth futures for ignoring climate crisis and imposing disproportionate cuts on young people, while proposing to reverse social security cuts, raise student grants, reject tuition fees, and guarantee work or study placements for unemployed youth.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
En ihmettele, että nuoret ovat myös Suomessa pettyneitä politiikkaan. Nuoret ovat olleet tälle hallitukselle vain toistuvien leikkausten kohde. Toivon, että me havahdumme nuorten hätään.
I'm not surprised that young people in Finland are also disappointed in politics. Young people have been only the target of repeated cuts for this government. I hope that we wake up to the distress of young people.
Pia Lohikoski
Me vasemmistoliitossa haluamme vahvistaa nuorten toimeentuloa perumalla tehdyt sosiaaliturvaleikkaukset ja korottamalla opintorahaa. Sanomme jyrkästi ei koulutuksen maksullisuudelle ja vaadimme työ- tai opiskelupaikkaa jokaiselle kolme kuukautta työttömänä olleelle nuorelle.
We in the Left Alliance want to strengthen young people's livelihood by reversing the social security cuts that have been made and raising student grants. We say a firm no to tuition fees and demand a work or study placement for every young person who has been unemployed for three months.
Pia Lohikoski

FR2026-10-23#
party document Mobilisation or protest

Nous dénonçons la fuite en avant autoritaire du pouvoir

France Unbowed calls for nationwide peaceful demonstrations alongside unions and student organizations against government protest bans, curfews, and what it characterizes as authoritarian measures, while supporting legal challenges to these restrictions.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Les libertés publiques ne peuvent pas être une variable d'ajustement d'un exécutif en perdition.
Public freedoms cannot be an adjustment variable for an executive in disarray.
France Unbowed
En décidant d'interdire des manifestations, de choisir qui a le droit ou pas d'en organiser, de mettre en place des couvre-feux à l'échelle d'un département ou d'une ville, d'inciter les commerçants à recourir à des sécurités privées, l'exécutif franchit une à une les limites qui séparent une démocratie d'un État autoritaire et policier.
By deciding to ban demonstrations, to choose who has the right or not to organize them, to implement curfews at the scale of a department or a city, to encourage merchants to use private security, the executive is crossing one by one the limits that separate a democracy from an authoritarian and police state.
France Unbowed

FR2026-10-22#
party document Policy proposal

La France insoumise à la Paris Games Week : pour une politique publique du jeu vidéo

France Unbowed announces a delegation of parliamentarians will attend Paris Games Week 2026 to present their video game policy program, including creation of a national video game center modeled on the national cinema center, funded by a tax on game sales.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
C'est pour ces raisons que nous souhaitons que la puissance publique reprenne la main sur ce secteur.
It is for these reasons that we want public authorities to take back control of this sector.
France Unbowed
Nous portons la création d'un centre national du jeu vidéo, sur le modèle du Centre national du cinéma, chargé notamment de soutenir la diversité et l'indépendance dans la création vidéoludique.
We support the creation of a national video game center, modeled on the National Cinema Center, responsible in particular for supporting diversity and independence in video game creation.
France Unbowed

party document Public statement

Trappes et La Verrière : le racisme tue encore une fois

The NPA-l'Anticapitaliste expressed solidarity with victims of a shooting in Trappes and La Verrière on October 23, where a far-right individual killed one Black man and seriously injured five others, including a 12-year-old girl, in what the party characterizes as a racist attack targeting people perceived as Arab and/or Muslim.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Ce vendredi 23 octobre, un individu d'extrême droite a tiré au pistolet mitrailleur à Trappes et à La Verrière, tuant un homme noir de 26 ans, Pengo Bossio Wangi, et en blessant grièvement cinq autres personnes, toutes visées parce que supposées arabes et/ou musulmanEs, dont une enfant de 12 ans, criblée de balles, visée parce que portant un foulard.
This Friday, October 23, a far-right individual fired a submachine gun in Trappes and La Verrière, killing a 26-year-old Black man, Pengo Bossio Wangi, and seriously injuring five other people, all targeted because they were assumed to be Arab and/or Muslim, including a 12-year-old child, riddled with bullets, targeted because she was wearing a headscarf.
New Anticapitalist Party – The Anticapitalist
Le NPA-l'Anticapitaliste exprime toute sa solidarité avec les victimes, leurs proches et les habitantEs.
The NPA-l'Anticapitaliste expresses its full solidarity with the victims, their loved ones and the residents.
New Anticapitalist Party – The Anticapitalist

Un couvre-feu autoritaire qui ne fera pas taire nos colères

The New Anticapitalist Party condemns curfews imposed in Paris, Marseille, Lyon, Toulouse, Nice, Lille, Bordeaux and other cities following mobilizations on October 17, describing them as a colonial method of order maintenance aimed at preventing mobilization and justifying repression.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

This colonial method of maintaining order has the sole purpose of preventing any mobilization and justifying even more brutal repression against those who refuse to be silenced.

GB2026-10-19#
party document Policy proposal

New Green Party transport policy aims for 75% cut in carbon emissions within ten years

The Green Party of England and Wales approved a revised transport policy at its Autumn Conference proposing a 75% reduction in carbon emissions within ten years through measures including a 50% increase in rail travel, making half of urban journeys active travel within five years, opposing new road building, and targeting private jets and frequent flyers.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
For too long transport policy has focused on moving vehicles rather than enabling people to get where they need to go. Our new policy is about giving everyone genuine choices – reliable and affordable public transport, safe walking, wheeling and cycling, and communities where owning a car isn't a prerequisite for participating fully in society.
Sian Berry
We also need to tackle transport's contribution to the climate crisis in an equitable and fair way. That means providing attractive alternatives to car and air travel, moving more freight onto rail and electrification. Those responsible for the most polluting and unnecessary journeys, including users of private jets and frequent flyers, will make the greatest contribution.
Sian Berry

Σοβαρές παρενέργειες μετά τα εμβόλια COVID-19 – Η κυβέρνηση οφείλει απαντήσεις στους ασθενείς

NIKI MP Aspasia Kouroupaki submitted parliamentary question 5130 on 6 May 2025 regarding government responsibility for serious COVID-19 vaccine adverse events, and proposes creating a nationwide specialized assessment network, scientific registry, and administrative compensation procedure for documented serious injuries.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Στις 6 Μαΐου 2025 κατέθεσα την Ερώτηση 5130 προς τους Υπουργούς Υγείας και Δικαιοσύνης, με αφορμή δικαστική απόφαση που επιδίκασε χρηματική ικανοποίηση 50.000 ευρώ σε πολίτη ο οποίος υπέστη σοβαρή νευρολογική βλάβη μετά τον εμβολιασμό του.
On 6 May 2025 I submitted Question 5130 to the Ministers of Health and Justice, prompted by a court decision that awarded monetary compensation of 50,000 euros to a citizen who suffered serious neurological damage after his vaccination.
Aspasia Kouroupaki
Προτείνω τη δημιουργία ενός πανελλαδικού δικτύου εξειδικευμένης αξιολόγησης και παρακολούθησης, με τη συμμετοχή πανεπιστημιακών νοσοκομείων, κατάλληλων κλινικών του ΕΣΥ και επιστημόνων από ειδικότητες όπως η καρδιολογία, η νευρολογία, η ανοσολογία και η αιματολογία.
I propose the creation of a nationwide network of specialized assessment and monitoring, with the participation of university hospitals, appropriate NHS clinics and scientists from specialties such as cardiology, neurology, immunology and hematology.
Aspasia Kouroupaki

Ρένα Δούρου: Ιστορική συμφωνία σε βάρος της Μονής Σινά

SYRIZA-PS parliamentary group president Rena Dourou criticized the Greek government's agreement with Egypt regarding Saint Catherine's Monastery in Sinai, demanding full disclosure of the agreement and questioning whether it converts historic property rights into mere usage tolerance.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Πράγματι η συμφωνία είναι ιστορική αλλά σε βάρος του αρχαιότερου χριστιανικού μοναστηριού σε αδιάκοπη λειτουργία εδώ και 15 αιώνες.
Indeed the agreement is historic but at the expense of the oldest Christian monastery in continuous operation for 15 centuries.
Rena Dourou
Ο Πρωθυπουργός και ο Υπουργός Εξωτερικών οφείλουν να δώσουν άμεσα στη δημοσιότητα το πλήρες κείμενο της συμφωνίας.
The Prime Minister and the Foreign Minister must immediately make public the full text of the agreement.
Rena Dourou

Cllr Conor Reddy Condemns Dubliner Ian Hutcheson’s Arrest and Detention by Israelis in the West Bank

People Before Profit councillor Conor Reddy condemned the arrest and detention of Dublin resident Ian Hutcheson by Israeli authorities in the West Bank, while TD Paul Murphy called for comprehensive sanctions against Israel and criticized the government for weakening the Occupied Territories Bill.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Each year, international volunteers travel to Palestine to provide international solidarity and act as observers as Palestinian farmers face harassment and attack by Israeli settlers and the Israeli authorities during the harvest season. Israeli settlers have carried out over 600 attacks in the West Bank since late September, including stealing olives and crops and cutting down and burning trees.
Conor Reddy
It is outrageous that Ian went to the West Bank to offer support and protection to Palestinians as they harvested their crops, and for this he was arrested and detained and appears set to be deported.
Conor Reddy

Zohran Mamdani Mayoral Campaign Organiser to address Derry Public Meeting

People Before Profit is hosting a public meeting in Derry on 21 October featuring Emma Bally, a coordinator from Zohran Mamdani's New York mayoral campaign, to discuss lessons from the socialist candidate's election victory.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
We're looking forward to hearing from Emma and the work of Democratic Socialist of American members in the Zohran Mamdani campaign. Mamdani's mayoral victory has created hope across the United States and internationally that change is possible. In a highly contested election, New Yorkers choose the pro-Palestine, pro-migrant, pro-right to choose, socialist candidate championing working-class demands, opposition to fascism and war. There are lessons for socialists, trade union activists and progressives in Ireland from the Mamdani campaign and the explosive growth of the socialist movement in the US. Mamdani is Mayor of the city that hosts Wall Street and many of the wealthiest people and corporations in the world. They will not want Mamdani to succeed or for a socialist progressive challenge to the Republican and Democratic Party to emerge.
Shaun Harkin

IE2026-10-20#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Government Denying Proper "End Triple Lock" Dáil Debate

People Before Profit TDs condemned the government's decision to limit debate on the Defence (Amendment) Bill 2026 ending the Triple Lock to 3.5 hours on Wednesday, opposed the time guillotine at an Oireachtas Business Committee meeting, and called for a referendum on neutrality and the triple lock.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
News that the Government will limit the debate on their legislation to end the Triple Lock confirms that Fianna Fáil and Fine Gael remain determined to dismantle our neutrality and align us ever more closely to the project of European militarisation and to NATO.
Richard Boyd Barrett
At an Oireachtas Business Committee meeting this morning I opposed the 'guillotine' to limit Wednesday's debate, but the Government would not agree to give additional time for an adequate debate on this defining issue.
Richard Boyd Barrett

IT2026-10-25#
party document Public statement

Cortei: misure governo garantiscono sicurezza e diritto a manifestare

Prime Minister Giorgia Meloni thanked security forces for managing demonstrations and defended recent Council of Ministers measures designed to give the state more effective tools to prevent violence during protests.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Voglio ringraziare le donne e gli uomini delle Forze dell'Ordine che anche oggi, nelle piazze italiane, hanno lavorato per garantire la sicurezza dei cittadini e il diritto di manifestare pacificamente, nonostante i tentativi di alcuni violenti di provocare scontri e disordini.
I want to thank the women and men of the Security Forces who today, in Italian squares, have worked to guarantee the security of citizens and the right to demonstrate peacefully, despite the attempts of some violent individuals to provoke clashes and disorder.
Giorgia Meloni
È in questa direzione che vanno anche le misure approvate nei giorni scorsi dal Consiglio dei Ministri: dare allo Stato strumenti più efficaci per prevenire le violenze e impedire che delinquenti e facinorosi possano mettere a ferro e fuoco intere città.
It is in this direction that the measures approved in recent days by the Council of Ministers also go: to give the State more effective tools to prevent violence and prevent criminals and troublemakers from putting entire cities to fire and sword.
Giorgia Meloni

party document Policy proposal

Olio: 16 milioni per sostenere produzioni Dop e Igp

The Ministry of Agriculture launches a tender through Agea to purchase at least 1.8 million liters of DOP and IGP extra virgin olive oil worth 16 million euros from European Social Fund Plus resources for food aid to the needy.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con questa misura mettiamo insieme due priorità del governo: sostenere le famiglie che vivono una condizione di difficoltà e valorizzare una delle eccellenze più rappresentative dell'agroalimentare italiano.
With this measure we bring together two government priorities: supporting families living in difficult conditions and enhancing one of the most representative excellences of Italian agri-food.
Francesco Lollobrigida
Con questo intervento mettiamo a valore circa 16 milioni di euro di fondi europei destinati al contrasto della povertà, trasformandoli in un aiuto concreto per le persone in difficoltà.
With this intervention we put to use about 16 million euros of European funds intended to combat poverty, transforming them into concrete help for people in difficulty.
Francesco Lollobrigida

IT2026-10-22#
party document Policy proposal

Carburanti: accordo con autotrasporto, risorse a agricoltura e pesca

The Italian government announced an agreement with transport associations providing immediate tax credit payments for diesel fuel from March-August 2026, allocated 60 million euros for agricultural diesel subsidies (up to 45 cents per liter discount), 10 million euros for fishing sector, and 106 million euros additional funding for September-October 2026 transport fuel credits, while warning that road blockades will be treated as criminal offenses under the Security Decree.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Il Governo, tramite il ministro Salvini, ha firmato in queste ultime ore un accordo con le associazioni dell'autotrasporto, con le quali ci siamo tra l'altro sempre confrontati e che ringrazio per la collaborazione, che non è mai mancata in questi mesi difficili.
The Government, through Minister Salvini, has signed in recent hours an agreement with the road transport associations, with whom we have always consulted and whom I thank for their collaboration, which has never been lacking in these difficult months.
Giorgia Meloni
Stanziamo 60 milioni di euro per un contributo a fondo perduto sul costo del gasolio agricolo, che nei fatti si tradurrà in uno sconto fino a 45 centesimi al litro, per oltre 120 milioni di litri.
We are allocating 60 million euros for a non-repayable contribution on the cost of agricultural diesel, which will in fact translate into a discount of up to 45 cents per liter, for over 120 million liters.
Giorgia Meloni

IT2026-10-21#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Protezione civile: riforma va incontro a esigenze territori

The Chamber definitively approved a civil protection reform bill introducing criminal liability discipline for volunteers, mayors, and operators, limiting responsibility to intentional crimes or gross negligence while excluding liability when guidelines are followed.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
La Camera oggi ha approvato in via definitiva un provvedimento che mi sta particolarmente a cuore: il disegno di legge sulla Protezione Civile. Una riforma storica, attesa da migliaia di volontari, che prevede l'introduzione della disciplina sulla responsabilità penale inerente i reati colposi accaduti durante le attività di protezione civile.
The Chamber today definitively approved a measure that is particularly close to my heart: the Civil Protection bill. A historic reform, awaited by thousands of volunteers, which provides for the introduction of discipline on criminal liability concerning negligent crimes that occurred during civil protection activities.
Marco Padovani
I volontari, così come i sindaci e gli operatori, risponderanno di reati dolosi o di colpa grave, ma sarà esclusa la responsabilità penale ogni volta che vengano osservate le linee guida o le buone pratiche.
Volunteers, as well as mayors and operators, will be liable for intentional crimes or gross negligence, but criminal liability will be excluded whenever guidelines or good practices are observed.
Marco Padovani

IT2026-10-20#
party document Policy proposal

Sicurezza: decreto governo risposta ferma contro violenti

Brothers of Italy announces a government security decree introducing criminal penalties for violent participation in demonstrations, parental liability for minor offenders, stricter accountability for 14-18 year-olds, and deployment of 500 military personnel to Milan following recent violent protests that injured nearly 200 police officers.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con il nuovo decreto sicurezza illustrato dal presidente Giorgia Meloni, il governo interviene con equilibrio ed efficacia per ristabilire il confine netto tra il legittimo dissenso – come quello espresso civilmente da categorie produttive quali agricoltori, autotrasportatori e lavoratori con cui l'Esecutivo si confronta costantemente – e la pura criminalità.
With the new security decree presented by President Giorgia Meloni, the government intervenes with balance and effectiveness to re-establish the clear boundary between legitimate dissent – such as that expressed civilly by productive categories like farmers, truck drivers and workers with whom the Executive constantly engages – and pure criminality.
Lucio Malan
Con il nuovo decreto sicurezza, tra l'altro, introduciamo il reato di partecipazione violenta a manifestazioni con pene fino a 5 anni di carcere e multe fino a 20mila euro, affermiamo il principio che chi rompe paga – con ingiunzioni dirette del Prefetto per i beni pubblici, decreti immediatamente esecutivi per i privati e responsabilità patrimoniale dei genitori per i minori –, interveniamo sull'imputabilità tra i 14 e i 18 anni estendendo il fermo preventivo ai minorenni e inviamo subito 500 militari in più a Milano fino a fine anno.
With the new security decree, among other things, we introduce the crime of violent participation in demonstrations with penalties up to 5 years in prison and fines up to 20,000 euros, we affirm the principle that whoever breaks pays – with direct injunctions from the Prefect for public property, immediately executable decrees for private property and parental financial liability for minors –, we intervene on accountability between 14 and 18 years by extending preventive detention to minors and we immediately send 500 more military personnel to Milan until the end of the year.
Galeazzo Bignami

IT2026-10-20#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Antimafia: depistaggi e indagini senza fondamento, Scarpinato si dimetta

Brothers of Italy parliamentarians demand the resignation of Senator Roberto Scarpinato from the Anti-Mafia Commission, citing testimony by Caltanissetta Prosecutor Salvatore De Luca that allegedly confirms Scarpinato conducted unfounded investigations in violation of procedural law and coordinated with an indicted colleague to obstruct commission work.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Definire 'intollerabile' e un 'salto di livello sproporzionato' e di inaudita virulenza l'attacco sferrato dal senatore Roberto Scarpinato contro il Gip del Tribunale di Caltanissetta, Santi Bologna certifica lo spettacolo indegno di chi, dopo aver sbandierato per una vita la difesa dei giudici dal potere politico, oggi usa il proprio scranno parlamentare per aggredire e delegittimare un giudice della Repubblica solo perché ha avuto il coraggio di scrivere la verità.
Defining as 'intolerable' and a 'disproportionate escalation' of unprecedented virulence the attack launched by Senator Roberto Scarpinato against the investigating judge of the Caltanissetta Court, Santi Bologna, certifies the unworthy spectacle of someone who, after having championed for a lifetime the defense of judges from political power, today uses his parliamentary seat to attack and delegitimize a judge of the Republic only because he had the courage to write the truth.
Raoul Russo
Il Procuratore ha chiarito in modo inequivocabile che per la Procura nissena il filone in sé non è chiuso e ha una sua dignità se sorretto da elementi individualizzanti, ma ciò che vale 'zero assoluto, meno 273,31 gradi della scala Kelvin' è proprio la specifica indagine portata avanti dall'allora Procuratore generale di Palermo Roberto Scarpinato
The Prosecutor clarified unequivocally that for the Caltanissetta Prosecutor's Office the line of investigation itself is not closed and has its dignity if supported by individualizing elements, but what is worth 'absolute zero, minus 273.31 degrees on the Kelvin scale' is precisely the specific investigation carried out by the then General Prosecutor of Palermo Roberto Scarpinato
Saverio Congedo

party document Parliamentary intervention

Covid: censurarono studio su effetti avversi al vaccino

Brothers of Italy parliamentarians Alice Buonguerrieri and Antonella Zedda used testimony in the Covid Commission to criticize alleged censorship of a 2022 study by ISS researcher Loredana Frasca on adverse vaccine effects, particularly myocarditis in young people, and questioned mass vaccination policies during the pandemic.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Il clima di caccia alle streghe durante la pandemia di Covid fu da regime totalitario. Giornalisti, esponenti politici, esperti di varie materie e persino ricercatori scientifici furono censurati, ridicolizzati o demonizzati per aver espresso opinioni e studi sgraditi al pensiero allora dominante.
The climate of witch-hunting during the Covid pandemic was like a totalitarian regime. Journalists, political figures, experts in various fields and even scientific researchers were censored, ridiculed or demonized for having expressed opinions and studies unwelcome to the then-dominant thinking.
Alice Buonguerrieri
A chi giovava questa vaccinazione di massa? Ne deve rispondere chi era al governo.
Who benefited from this mass vaccination? Those who were in government must answer for it.
Alice Buonguerrieri

The Italian Communist Party condemns the arrest warrant issued by Florida's attorney general against Raul Castro, expresses solidarity with Cuba and Venezuela, and calls for mobilization in Italy to defend the Cuban Revolution against what it characterizes as US aggression and economic blockade.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

Krever svar om Ringeriksbanen

Progress Party MP Morten Wold demands answers from the new transport minister about the Ringerike Railway after the government budget proposal allocated no funds for further planning of the Ringerike Railway/New Bergen Railway and new E16 to Hønefoss.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Jeg har gang på gang forsøkt å få tidligere samferdselsminister Jon-Ivar Nygård (Ap) til å få fortgang på videre framdrift rundt Ringeriksbanen/FRE16, eller Bergensbanans forkortelse som vi også kaller det, men Nygård og Ap har aldri hatt noe ønske om å realisere prosjektet. Derfor prøver jeg nå å få svar fra den nye samferdselsministeren.
I have repeatedly tried to get the former transport minister Jon-Ivar Nygård (Labour Party) to make progress on further advancement around the Ringerike Railway/FRE16, or the Bergen Railway shortcut as we also call it, but Nygård and Labour have never had any desire to realize the project. Therefore I am now trying to get answers from the new transport minister.
Morten Wold

party document Parliamentary intervention

Vil heller styrke ordningen

Progress Party MP Julia Brännström Nordtug opposes the government's proposal to reduce the number of activity aids available to people over 26 with disabilities, proposing instead to strengthen the scheme with a scaling-up plan and a return or deposit system for unused aids.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Dette handler om voksne mennesker som vil komme seg ut. Det er foreldre som vil følge barna sine på skitur om vinteren, og på sykkeltur om sommeren. Det må de få lov til
This is about adults who want to get out. There are parents who want to accompany their children on ski trips in winter, and on bike trips in summer. They must be allowed to do that
Julia Brännström Nordtug
Vi kommer ikke til å støtte regjeringens forslag på dette. Vi vil heller se på muligheter for å innføre en returordning eller en panteordning, som kan sørge for at vi får tak i de hjelpemidlene som ligger og støver bort, men som kan være til nytte for andre
We will not support the government's proposal on this. We would rather look at opportunities to introduce a return scheme or a deposit scheme, which can ensure that we get hold of the aids that are gathering dust, but which can be useful for others
Julia Brännström Nordtug

NO2026-10-19#
party document Policy proposal

Tannhelsereformen: Får nye rødgrønne ben å gå på!

The Socialist Left Party (SV) announces that a comprehensive dental health reform proposal is being put out for consultation, building on five years of budget negotiations with Labour and Centre parties that have already given over 550,000 Norwegians cheaper dental care, with the proposal including free necessary orthodontics for children and phased expansion of dental rights starting with younger age groups.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
For SV har det lenge vært klart at tennene er en del av kroppen, da skal ikke ommeboka avgjøre om du får den helsehjelpa du trenger.
For SV it has long been clear that teeth are part of the body, so the wallet should not determine whether you get the healthcare you need.
Kirsti Bergstø
Dette har vi kjempa for siden 2004, og endelig sier flertallet at det ikke lenger skal koste mer å gå til tannlegen enn til legen
We have fought for this since 2004, and finally the majority says it should no longer cost more to go to the dentist than to the doctor
Kirsti Bergstø

Partido Comunista Português

PCP Secretary-General Paulo Raimundo condemned government housing policies and announced the party will propose measures in the state budget to regulate and lower rents, guarantee 10-year rental contracts, invest 1% of GDP annually in public construction, lower banking spreads and commissions, strengthen housing cooperatives, and reform land laws.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

The indignation, protest and rejection expressed in the CGTP demonstration last Saturday and in the actions that have taken place in recent days, particularly in Lisbon and Porto, against a policy that measure after measure clearly denies the right to housing, is justified.
Paulo Raimundo
PSD, CDS, Chega e IL, porta-vozes dos fundos imobiliários e testas de ferro da especulação e dos especuladores querem agora dar ainda mais poder à grande propriedade, querem acelerar ainda mais os despejos, querem aumentar ainda mais as rendas

PT2026-10-19#
party document Alliance or coordination

Partido Comunista Português

PCP Secretary-General Paulo Raimundo announced at a press conference following the Central Committee meeting of October 18-19, 2026, that the party will launch a national campaign in early November under the slogan "Children and Parents with rights. Portugal with future" and outlined policy proposals including ten-year rental contracts, 1% GDP investment in public housing, raising minimum wage to 1100 euros, and increasing all pensions by 80 euros.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
É urgente travar a política injusta e promotora das desigualdades que condena a maioria às dificuldades ao mesmo tempo que é o garante da concentração da riqueza nas mãos dos que se acham donos disto tudo.
It is urgent to stop the unjust policy that promotes inequalities, which condemns the majority to difficulties while guaranteeing the concentration of wealth in the hands of those who think they own everything.
Paulo Raimundo
O Comité Central do PCP decidiu, no seguimento de uma linha de trabalho da maior importância, levar por diante a partir de inicio de Novembro uma acção com expressão nacional sob o lema "Crianças e Pais com direitos. Portugal com futuro", uma prioridade de intervenção que o PCP assume.
The PCP Central Committee decided, following a line of work of the greatest importance, to carry forward from the beginning of November a national action under the slogan "Children and Parents with rights. Portugal with future", a priority intervention that the PCP assumes.
Paulo Raimundo
